- **taiko-x402**: x402 HTTP payment integration specialist for Taiko. Builds payment-protected API endpoints (sellers) and x402-enabled clients or AI agents (buyers) using Taiko's facilitators. Triggers: "x402", "HTTP payment", "pay per request", "monetize API", "USDC payment", "402 Payment Required", "facilitator.taiko.xyz", "paid API", "AI agent payments", "payment middleware". Use proactively when adding payment gating to APIs on Taiko or building clients that pay for x402 services. ([taikoxyz/taiko-ai](https://github.com/taikoxyz/taiko-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/taiko-x402.md) · ★1)

([jpatrickb/jobtracker](https://github.com/jpatrickb/jobtracker/blob/HEAD/agents/tailor-application.md) · 3 copies)
- **tailscale-wif-setup**: Sets up and troubleshoots secretless GitHub Actions -&gt; Tailscale auth via Workload Identity Federation (WIF) for a given repo and workflow. Computes the ID-embedded OIDC subject, walks the user through creating the Tailscale Trust Credential in the admin console, wires the two public repo variables, edits the workflow to drop the stored credential, and diagnoses 403 / tags / auth-mode failures. Use when a workflow needs to talk to Tailscale without a stored API key or OAuth secret, or when an existing WIF workflow is failing. ([JRichlen/agent-plugins](https://github.com/JRichlen/agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/tailscale-wif/agents/tailscale-wif-setup.md) · ★1)

- **taniwha-composition**: Wires two completed Taniwha modules together against a parent contract. ([taniwhaai/arai](https://github.com/taniwhaai/arai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/taniwha-composition.md) · ★7 · 2 copies)
- **taniwha-contract-derivation**: Derives per-module contracts (manifests) and shared vocabulary from a Taniwha design document. Returns manifests, shared vocabulary, or a re-raise. Do not invoke directly. ([taniwhaai/arai](https://github.com/taniwhaai/arai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/taniwha-contract-derivation.md) · ★7 · 2 copies)

- **taniwha-leaf-implementation**: Implements a single Taniwha module against its contract manifest. Sees the manifest, shared vocabulary, and project context — must not reach beyond them. Returns implementation files, tests, manifest, and notes — or a re-raise. Do not invoke directly. - **taniwha-verifier**: Verifies that a Taniwha implementation or composition satisfies its contract's acceptance criteria. Invoked by the Taniwha dispatcher after every leaf-implementation or composition produces output and before the orchestrator marks the work current. Returns a verifier_report or a re-raise. Do not invoke directly. ([taniwhaai/arai](https://github.com/taniwhaai/arai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/taniwha-verifier.md) · ★7 · 2 copies)

- **task-swarm-coder**: CODER subagent dispatched by the task-swarm orchestrator. Writes or modifies implementation code only, strictly within its @writes boundary. - **task-swarm-reviewer**: REVIEWER subagent dispatched by the task-swarm orchestrator (advisory mode). Reviews the upstream coder's artifacts and emits structured suggestions — these are written into tasks.md as `&gt; ⚠️` comments for the user to review. **Does not participate in the fix loop** — the reviewer never fails a stage and never re-dispatches a coder; the validator is the only blocking gate. Has no Edit/Write tools — code changes are forbidden at the tool level. Invoked only by the orchestrator within the task-swarm flow. ([qxbyte/pluginhub](https://github.com/qxbyte/pluginhub/blob/HEAD/plugins/task-swarm/agents/task-swarm-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **task-swarm-validator**: VALIDATOR subagent forked by the task-swarm orchestrator. Invoked only inside the task-swarm flow by the lead orchestrator. ([qxbyte/pluginhub](https://github.com/qxbyte/pluginhub/blob/HEAD/plugins/task-swarm/agents/task-swarm-validator.md) · ★3)
